HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000259/B010","root_000286/B005","root_000782/B003","root_000901/B001","root_000993/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_1306f4eeb5471b3a025c","text":"3. **Charge mirrored inward.** Evidence: ش د د B003 forceful charge/running; ع د و B002 running; ض ب ح B001 breath; ج م ع B010 gathering force; ح ب ب B005 camel fixed by exhaustion. Structural cue: the opening kinetic sequence returns in the moral predicate \"shadid.\" Role: shiddah carries both martial force and hardened intensity; hubb can immobilize like an exhausted animal. Abductive move: the same force that drives the runners also knots the human inwardly, producing both charge and stuckness. Reading change: the verse mirrors the opening: the human is a battlefield of desire's charge.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000452/B005","root_000782/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_227baeb920874bb7fadb","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"100:8:3\",\"root_000452\",[\"B005\"]],[\"100:8:4\",\"root_000782\",[\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"İyilik veya armağan olarak dolaşması gereken mal, şiddetli tutunma yüzünden elde tutulup başkasına aktarılmaz.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000286/B001","root_000286/B004","root_000330/B002","root_000452/B002","root_000452/B003","root_000849/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2ad81cb01545f71a0245","text":"2. **Seed/kernel economy.** Evidence: ح ب ب B001 seed-grain/kernel and B004 heart's inner kernel; خ ي ر B002 choice excellence and B003 choosing; ح ص ل B002 extracting kernel/value; ص د ر B001 chest. Structural cue: later 100:10 will extract what is in breasts. Role: hubb supplies both love and kernel; khayr supplies selected value; tahsil later exposes the kernel. Abductive move: desire has an inner seed lodged in the chest that later extraction will sort. Reading change: the ayah becomes a statement about the heart's concealed kernel of valuation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000286/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_443f88db338b31a9eecd","text":"1. **Love as a fastened bond.** `ح ب ب B002` supplies love as clinging attachment, and `ش د د B001` supplies tightening or fastening a bond. The construction places `حُبِّ ٱلْخَيْرِ` before the emphatic predicate `لَشَدِيدٌ`, so object, attachment, and fastening reinforce one another. Love is the bond and severity is its tightening force. The packet supplies both roles; I infer that intensity operates by making separation difficult. The line reads as structural bondage to the desired good, not only as a high degree on an emotional scale.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4ff2fcd75d0e0963afb9","text":"100:10 was decisive: حب as kernel/heart-core became stronger once \"what is in the breasts\" was extracted. 100:1-5 also looped back through شدة, making desire a continuation of the opening charge rather than a separate moral aside.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000286/B001","root_000286/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_51a3d37ae8144c80e132","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"100:8:2\",\"root_000286\",[\"B001\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Sevginin göğüste saklı bir çekirdek oluşu, ileride ayıklanıp gerçek değeri veya artığı belirlenecek iç yönelimi gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000286/B002","root_000452/B001","root_000452/B005","root_000782/B001","root_000782/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_5fe13406553bbc50af57","text":"1. **Attachment hardened into restraint.** Evidence: ح ب ب B002 love/clinging attachment; خ ي ر B001 desired good and B005 beneficence/gift; ش د د B001 fastening a bond and B002 intensity/hardness. Structural cue: repeated \"and indeed he\" ties witness to intense love. Role: love attaches; \"good\" is the desired object; شد makes the attachment hard and binding. Abductive move: what should circulate as beneficence becomes privately fastened to the self. Reading change: the ayah reads as possessive fixation on benefit, not simply liking wealth/good.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000286/B001","root_000286/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_654e47446afad2d3eb1a","text":"3. **The loved good is lodged as an inner kernel whose yield will be tested.** `ح ب ب B001` supplies seed-grain and `B004` the heart's inner kernel. The earlier `ك ن د B003` in 100:6 supplies barren land, while 100:10 uses `ح ص ل B002` to extract a kernel or valuable substance from its covering and places that operation in the breasts. These branches create a planted kernel, its receiving ground, and later extraction or assessment. The packet does not equate grammatical `حُبّ` with a seed; that material overlay is my abductive analogy.
